The proposed architecture allocates patients to specialized risk predictors dynamically while preserving the patient survival and subtype clustering objectives. We compare our method with state-of-the-art survival and deep clustering models on multiple real-world longitudinal clinical cohorts spanning diverse disease domains. The proposed method demonstrates improved predictive performance and leads to interpretable results in survival analysis.

</details>


### [164] [Code Correctness Signals in LLM Hidden States: Pre-Generation Probing and Repair Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14530)
*Carlo Di Cicco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507在代码生成和修复过程中，隐藏状态是否编码了代码正确性信息。研究发现：1) 首次尝试代码的正确性可从生成前的隐藏状态线性解码（AUC 0.931）；2) 在修复失败的代码时，隐藏状态变化中的对比方向在控制修复上下文协变量后消失，表明其并非真正的修复理解特征。本文同时报告了正向和负向结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型在代码生成和修复过程中，其隐藏状态是否及如何编码代码正确性信息，特别关注在生成之前和修复失败尝试时的可读性。

Method: 使用残差化（residualization）工具，在444个LiveCodeBench任务上研究Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507模型。对首次尝试：从提示最终隐藏状态线性解码正确性，并控制提示长度混杂效应。对修复尝试：分析从失败尝试到修复尝试的隐藏状态偏移，进行条件残差化控制修复上下文协变量。

Result: 首次尝试代码的正确性线性可解码AUC为0.931±0.008，残差化后为0.911±0.010（高于提示长度基线0.754±0.014）。修复方向在统计上可检测，但条件残差化后不显著，表明是修复上下文协变量的相关物而非独立的修复理解特征。

Conclusion: 预生成阶段隐藏状态确实编码代码正确性信息，但修复阶段的隐藏状态变化方向并非真正的修复理解特征，而是修复上下文协变量的副产品。方法贡献与实证贡献并重：诚实地报告了正向结果和负向结果。

Abstract: Large language models encode rich information in their hidden states. This work asks whether code correctness is legible in the hidden states of Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507, before it generates and as it repairs a failed attempt, studied on 444 LiveCodeBench tasks. It reports two findings connected by a single confound-control tool: residualization. First, the correctness of the model's first-attempt code is linearly decodable from the prompt-final hidden state, with a leakage-free held-out AUC of 0.931 +/- 0.008 across 50 outer splits. After the linear effect of prompt length is removed from each hidden state dimension, the probe still reaches 0.911 +/- 0.010, well above a prompt-length baseline of 0.754 +/- 0.014. Second, on 236 cleaned cases where the model attempts to repair a failed first attempt, the hidden state shift from the failing attempt to its repair carries a statistically detectable contrastive direction, significant on both a magnitude and a split-half test against label-shuffled nulls. This direction does not survive a conditional residualization against repair-context covariates that differ between successful and failed repairs, marking it as a correlate of repair success driven by the repair context rather than an isolated repair-comprehension feature. The probe layer is selected by nested cross-validation, and the same residualization approach that upholds the pre-generation correctness result overturns the repair-direction interpretation. The contribution is as much methodological as empirical: a diagnostic honest enough to report a negative result alongside a positive one.

</details>


### [165] [The Risk Shadow of Principal Component Analysis: When 99.9999% Variance Preservation Causes Catastrophic Decision Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14533)
*Hamidou Tembine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明PCA在保留总方差时可能完全抹去罕见灾难事件的关键信号（即"风险阴影"），并提出ExPCA和TP-PCA两种重新加权协方差以保留尾部风险信息的方法，在理论和实验上证明其优于PCA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PCA以方差最大化为目标，但方差保留与罕见高风险事件的信号保留之间存在根本性不匹配，可能导致高维数据降维后完全丢失对灾难性事件（如欺诈、故障）的检测能力，这对高风险决策场景构成严重威胁。

Method: 1) 理论证明PCA存在"风险阴影"——可保留超过99.9999%总方差却完全抹去罕见事件信号；2) 提出Expectile PCA (ExPCA) 和 Tail-Preserving PCA (TP-PCA)，通过重新加权数据协方差矩阵，使降维方向更关注尾部高影响事件。

Result: 理论证明ExPCA在保留罕见事件信息上严格优于PCA；在合成数据和真实信用卡欺诈检测基准上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 高赌注决策中应从根本上重新审视基于方差的降维方法，ExPCA和TP-PCA为保留尾部风险信息提供了可行的替代方案。

Abstract: Principal Component Analysis (PCA) preserves variance, not the information needed to detect rare catastrophic events. This paper proves the existence of a {\it Risk Shadow}: PCA can retain over 99.9999 percent of total variance while completely erasing all signal about rare, high-impact failures. When this happens, even the best possible classifier operating on the PCA representation reduces to a constant predictor. The root cause is a fundamental mismatch between variance maximization and tail risk awareness. To break the shadow, we introduce Expectile PCA (ExPCA) and Tail-Preserving PCA (TP-PCA), two methods that reweight the data covariance toward high-impact events. We prove theoretically that ExPCA strictly outperforms PCA in retaining rare-event information, and we validate our claims on synthetic data and a real-world credit card fraud detection benchmark. Our results call for a fundamental rethinking of variance-based dimensionality reduction in high-stakes decisions.

</details>


### [166] [Provably Safe, Yet Scalable Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14536)
*Kai S. Yun,Zeyang Li,Navid Azizan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PS2-RL框架，一种两阶段架构，通过隐式控制不变集学习可证明安全的强化学习策略，解决了现有方法在可扩展性和保守性方面的瓶颈，在高达10维的机器人控制任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习方法存在两难困境：软约束方法无严格安全保证，而具有严格保证的方法依赖显式证书函数构建控制不变集，难以扩展到高维状态空间且行为过于保守。

Method: PS2-RL采用两阶段架构：第一阶段训练备份策略，通过提出的安全到达价值函数生成隐式控制不变集；第二阶段通过可微投影层端到端训练RL策略，严格强制执行备份策略诱导的安全保证。

Result: 在高达10维状态的机器人控制任务上验证，PS2-RL在保持可证明安全性的同时具有良好性能和可扩展性，而此前可证明安全的RL方法在此维度下难以实用。

Conclusion: PS2-RL框架不限制底层RL算法，可插入任意现有训练流程，为可证明安全且可扩展的强化学习提供了有效范式。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) aims to learn policies that optimize rewards while satisfying constraints. Predominant approaches rely on soft-constrained policy optimization, which has achieved empirical success but does not provide formal safety guarantees for the learned policy. In contrast, methods with strict guarantees typically rely on explicit certificate functions, whose construction requires the direct synthesis and verification of control-invariant sets, a process that scales poorly with state dimension and often yields overly conservative behavior. In this paper, we present the Provably Safe, yet Scalable RL (PS2-RL) framework, a novel two-phase architecture for learning provably safe policies in a scalable manner, designed to overcome the key bottlenecks of prior methods. Rather than explicitly computing invariant sets, PS2-RL leverages a learned backup policy to forward-integrate the system dynamics, generating an implicit control-invariant set online. In the first phase, the backup policy is trained with our proposed safe-arrival value function, which characterizes the optimal backup policy for invariant-set construction. In the second phase, an RL policy is trained end-to-end through a differentiable projection layer that strictly enforces the safety guarantees induced by the learned backup policy. By maximizing the volume of the implicit control-invariant set in the first phase, the resulting PS2 policy from the second phase is performant and scalable, while maintaining provable safety. Crucially, PS2-RL imposes no restrictions on the underlying RL algorithm and can be plugged into any existing training pipeline. We establish theoretical guarantees for the proposed framework and evaluate it on robotic control tasks with state dimensions up to 10, a regime in which prior provably safe RL methods struggle or become impractical.

</details>


### [167] [Zero-shot generalization of transformer neural operators to larger domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14597)
*Armand de Villeroché,Sibo Cheng,Vincent Le Guen,Marc Bocquet,Rem-Sophia Mouradi,Patrick Armand,Alban Farchi,Patrick Massin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过可分解的注意力对数计算偏置与旋转位置嵌入相结合的方法，使基于Transformer的神经算子能够零样本泛化到训练时未见的大尺度空间域上，在多个PDE基准和3D工业大气流应用上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的神经算子隐式假设固定域大小，无法在推理时泛化到更大的空间域上，这限制了其在复杂几何上求解PDE的实际应用能力。

Method: 在注意力对数计算中引入可分解的偏置来实现空间局部性，支持细粒度可控的局部性同时保持与查询-键内积的完全可分解性，兼容优化后的注意力核；结合旋转位置嵌入（RoPE）以支持可控空间范围的可表达嵌入，且不改变Transformer架构。

Result: 在两个PDE基准和一个3D工业大气流应用上，该方法显著提升了向更大域进行零样本泛化的性能。

Conclusion: 通过可分解局部性偏置与旋转位置嵌入的组合，能够在不修改Transformer架构的前提下有效实现域扩展，为神经算子在更大空间域上的零样本推理提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Transformer-based neural operators have shown remarkable performance for approximating solution operators of partial differential equations on complex geometries. However, existing approaches implicitly assume a fixed domain size, which limits their ability to generalize at inference. In this work, we investigate domain extension, namely zero-shot inference on spatial domains that are significantly larger than those encountered during training. We argue that this setting fundamentally requires spatial locality and translation equivariance. We propose to implement this locality via a decomposable bias in the attention logits computation, enabling finely controllable locality while remaining fully decomposable into query-key inner products and directly compatible with optimized attention kernels. Combined with rotary positional embeddings, it enables expressive embeddings with controllable spatial support without altering the transformer architecture. We empirically show that our approach substantially improves zero-shot generalization to larger domains across two PDE benchmarks and a 3D industrial atmospheric flow application. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/cerea-daml/domain-extension.

</details>


### [168] [Realizing Native INT8 Compute for Diffusion Transformers on Consumer GPUs: A Fused INT8 GEMM Kernel for Ideogram 4.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14598)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对扩散Transformer的后训练INT8量化在消费级Ampere GPU上实际慢于FP8和NF4，原因是现有实现仅做量化后立即反量化回bf16运算，未利用INT8张量核心。本文通过融合Triton INT8 GEMM内核（int8×int8→int32，含per-token×per-channel反量化和bias融合），在Ideogram 4.0扩散Transformer上实现2.8-4.2x每GEMM加速，端到端约1.1x加速，使INT8从最慢变为最快方案，1024px单卡RTX 3090上仅156.5秒，优于NF4（164.5s）和FP8（172.9s），且无质量损失。但该优势仅限于消费级Ampere，在A100/B200上不如原生bf16/FP8路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练INT8量化本应加速扩散Transformer推理，但在消费级Ampere GPU上实际速度慢于FP8和NF4方案，原因是现有实现仅做量化后立即反量化回bf16进行矩阵乘，未利用GPU的INT8张量核心，导致硬件的计算优势完全未被发挥。

Method: 设计一个融合的Triton INT8 GEMM内核：在Ampere张量核心上执行int8×int8→int32累加，在epilogue中融合per-token×per-channel反量化和bias加法，并按GEMM形状自动调优。将该内核替换Ideogram 4.0扩散Transformer线性层中的dequantize-to-bf16路径。

Result: 内核级别：int8×int8→int32累加与torch._int_mm比特精确，反量化输出余弦相似度1.0且无NaN，每GEMM比bf16快2.8-4.2x。端到端：768px下约1.1x加速（~9-10%）；1024px下单卡RTX 3090上156.5秒，优于NF4（164.5s）和FP8（172.9s），无质量损失（PickScore/CLIPScore不变）。INT8从最慢变最快。

Conclusion: INT8在消费级Ampere GPU上通过正确利用张量核心可实现显著加速，超越FP8和NF4方案，使1024px单卡可行。但该优势具有硬件特异性：在A100和B200上该内核输于这些卡的原生bf16/FP8路径。主要目标（beat FP8，~9.5%）达标；NF4 margin（~4.9%）在单次运行方差内，可视为接近拉伸目标。

Abstract: Post-training INT8 (W8A8) quantization of diffusion transformers is widely deployed as a speed optimization, yet on consumer Ampere GPUs it is frequently slower than the FP8 and NF4 alternatives it is meant to beat. We trace this to a software artifact: the production "INT8" forward quantizes weights and activations only to immediately dequantize them back to bf16 and run a bf16 matrix multiply, never engaging the GPU's INT8 tensor cores, so the hardware's compute advantage is left entirely unrealized. We close this gap with a single fused Triton INT8 GEMM (int8xint8->int32 on Ampere tensor cores, with per-token x per-channel dequantization and bias folded into the epilogue, autotuned per GEMM shape) dropped into the Ideogram 4.0 diffusion transformer's linear layers in place of the dequantize-to-bf16 path. In the kernel, the int8xint8->int32 accumulation is bit-exact against torch._int_mm and the dequantized output matches the reference at cosine similarity 1.0 with no NaNs, running 2.8-4.2x faster than bf16 per GEMM. End to end it delivers a ~1.1x (~9-10%) speedup at 768px, and at 1024px it generates an image in 156.5 s on a single RTX 3090, faster than the single-card NF4 (164.5 s) and FP8 (172.9 s) baselines, at no measurable quality cost on these point estimates (PickScore/CLIPScore). INT8 thus goes from the slowest variant to the fastest, and 1024px becomes single-GPU feasible. The primary speed criterion (beat FP8, by ~9.5%) is comfortably met; the NF4 margin (~4.9%, single-run n=4) is within run-to-run variance we did not quantify and is best read as consistent with meeting the stretch target. We close with an honest deployment map: the win is specific to consumer Ampere, and on A100 and B200 the same kernel loses to those cards' fast native bf16/FP8 paths.

</details>


### [169] [Neither Parallel Nor Sequential: How DiffusionGemma Actually Commits Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14620)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过系统性测量发现，DiffusionGemma 26B的离散扩散解码并非真正的并行或块自回归，而是存在弱左到右提交偏置，其"块大小"是测量粒度的伪影而非架构属性；模型在大型批量中同时提交token，批量内顺序本质上是未定义的，且行为具有任务依赖性。核心贡献在于提出了诚实验证解码顺序的方法论，揭示了多种可能制造假象的混淆因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放扩散语言模型被宣传为并行非自回归解码器，但实际检查点提交token的顺序几乎从未被测量，导致对其解码行为缺乏经验性理解。

Method: 对DiffusionGemma 26B（基于Gemma 4的掩码离散扩散混合专家模型）的采样器接受步骤进行hook，记录每个画布位置的提交时间与置信度；在686个提示、六种场景的探测套件下系统分析解码顺序。

Result: 解码既非并行也非块自回归；存在弱左到右偏置，其强度依赖分析粒度；模型以大批量同步提交token，批量内顺序未定义；行为具有任务依赖性（结构化JSON顺序任意，数学推理中置信度与正确性相关但事实回忆无此信号）；提交进度激进，在预算内短时爆发完成；任务准确率与自回归Gemma 4相当。

Conclusion: 诚实验证解码顺序必须处理尾部EOS填充、场景内混杂、提交非单调性、块大小敏感性和大批提交平局等混淆因素，否则容易得出虚假的解码顺序结论。

Abstract: Open diffusion language models are marketed as parallel, non-autoregressive decoders, yet the order in which a shipped checkpoint actually commits its tokens is almost never measured. We instrument DiffusionGemma 26B, a masked discrete-diffusion mixture-of-experts model built on Gemma 4, hooking its sampler's accept step to record which canvas positions commit, when, and at what confidence. Across a 686-prompt, six-regime probe suite we find that its decoding is neither parallel nor block-autoregressive: it follows a partial left-to-right commit bias whose apparent strength depends almost entirely on the granularity at which you look. Order is weak token by token and strengthens smoothly as the analysis is coarsened, so the model's "block size" turns out to be an artifact of the measuring ruler rather than the architecture. The model commits in large simultaneous batches, leaving much of the within-batch order genuinely undefined rather than merely unobserved. The behaviour is regime-dependent: structured JSON is committed in essentially arbitrary order, and a position's commit confidence tracks correctness on mathematical reasoning but carries no signal on factual recall. Commitment is aggressive, finishing in a short late burst well inside the step budget, while task accuracy matches the model's autoregressive Gemma-4 sibling. Beyond these findings, our central contribution is methodological: measuring decoding order honestly demands handling trailing-EOS padding, within-regime confounding, commit non-monotonicity, block-size sensitivity, and large commit-batch ties, each of which can otherwise manufacture a decoding-order result that is not really there.

</details>


### [170] [Graph Diffusion Residuals for Control-Function Instrumental Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14636)
*Rui Wu,Zongyuan Chen,Hong Xie,Defu Lian,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应各向异性仪器热流（A-IHF），一种用于灵活控制函数的确定性图扩散残差提取器。A-IHF将处理变量视为一阶段特征图上的信号，通过引导扩散检测处理跳跃、衰减跨跳跃导度，并利用稀疏图预解式计算生成的控制函数。其观测选择规则仅使用(Z,X)，结合图广义交叉验证、粗糙度、残差化处理相关性和图可容许性过滤。理论分析将误差分解为结构泄漏、残差衰减和残差化处理变异，给出有限样本界限和图可容许性速率。在54个合成基准单元上，受保护的观测A-IHF在平均结构响应MSE上最低；A-IHF家族在32个单元中优于最佳非A-IHF基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制函数工具变量估计器需要一阶段残差而非一阶段预测。高容量一阶段模型可能插值处理变量，导致结果方程中残留信息不足。现有方法在处理高维或非线性一阶段结构时面临残差提取的挑战，亟需一种能够有效提取残差同时保留处理变异的方法。

Method: A-IHF将处理变量作为一阶段特征图上的信号，通过各向异性图扩散过程提取残差：首先进行引导扩散检测处理变量的跳跃点，然后衰减这些跳跃处的导度，最后通过稀疏图预解式计算生成的控制函数。选择规则结合图广义交叉验证、粗糙度、残差化处理相关性和图可容许性过滤，仅使用(Z,X)进行观测选择。

Result: 在54个合成基准单元中，受保护的观测A-IHF在平均结构响应MSE上表现最佳；A-IHF家族在32个单元中优于最佳非A-IHF基线。性能在图捕捉到分段平滑一阶段结构时最强。理论分析提供了有限样本误差界限和图可容许性速率。

Conclusion: A-IHF作为一种确定性图扩散残差提取器，在灵活控制函数框架下有效解决了高容量一阶段模型插值导致的残差不足问题，尤其在图结构能够捕捉分段平滑一阶段特征时表现卓越。

Abstract: Control-function instrumental variable estimators need a first-stage residual, not merely a first-stage prediction. High-capacity first stages can interpolate treatment and leave too little residual information for the outcome equation. We study Adaptive Anisotropic Instrumental Heat Flow (A-IHF), a deterministic graph-diffusion residual extractor for flexible control functions. A-IHF treats treatment as a signal on a graph of first-stage features, uses pilot diffusion to detect large treatment jumps, attenuates conductance across those jumps, and computes the generated control with a sparse graph resolvent. Its observational selection rule uses only $(Z,X)$, combining graph generalized cross-validation, roughness, residualized-treatment relevance, and graph-admissibility filtering. The analysis decomposes error into structural leakage, residual attenuation, and residualized treatment variation, yielding finite-sample bounds, graph-admissibility rates under latent piecewise-smooth geometry, and finite-path selection calibration. Across 54 synthetic benchmark cells with tuned graph, kernel, tree, boosting, series, and neural control-function baselines, guarded observational A-IHF has the lowest average structural-response MSE; the A-IHF family beats the best non-A-IHF baseline in 32 cells. Performance is strongest when the graph captures piecewise-smooth first-stage structure.

</details>


### [171] [Online Convex Optimization with Sublinear Noisy Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14640)
*Simone Di Gregorio,Anupam Gupta,Stefano Leonardi,Matteo Russo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线凸优化中引入有预算的成对探测（pairwise probing）机制，证明即使次线性且带噪声的探测预算也能显著提升最坏情况下的遗憾界，并给出了紧的上下界分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有在线凸优化（OCO）研究中有两股独立工作流：专家问题中的次线性最优专家查询，以及OCO中每轮可用的成对比较反馈。本文希望提出统一框架，在OCO中引入有限次成对探测，探究其能否以及如何改善全反馈下的最坏情况遗憾。

Method: 提出统一探测模型：学习者在T轮中仅有k次成对探测预算（k≤T），每次可查询两个点并获知哪一点损失更小（可能带有δ噪声）。结合连续指数权重（Continuous Exponential Weights）的二阶（基于方差）分析，将探测的收益量化为方差缩减效应，推导出遗憾上界。

Result: 得到遗憾界 Reg_T ≤ O(min{√(dT ln T), dT ln T/(k|1-2δ|)})，该界在T、k和δ上紧（至多对数因子）。当δ接近1/2（即噪声接近随机抛硬币）时，遗憾性能平滑退化。在有限集（d个专家）场景下，所得速率在所有参数上完全紧。

Conclusion: 证明了即使次线性且含噪声的成对探测预算也能在最坏情况下改善全反馈OCO的遗憾界，为带探测的在线学习提供了统一的理论分析框架，并且通过方差缩减视角揭示了探测的内在效益。

Abstract: We study Online Convex Optimization (OCO) over a convex set $K\subseteq \mathbb R^d$, where in each round $t$ the learner selects $x_t\in K$ and then observes a convex loss $f_t:K\to[0,1]$, with the goal of minimizing regret to the best fixed decision in hindsight. We introduce a unified probing model that generalizes two recent lines of work: sublinear best-expert queries in the experts setting, and pairwise (comparison-based) feedback available every round in OCO. In our framework, the learner has a budget of $k\le T$ pairwise probes; on a probed round it may query two points and learn which one has smaller loss.
  Our main result shows that even a sublinear and noisy probe budget can provably improve worst-case regret in the full feedback OCO regime. With $k$ $δ$-noisy pairwise probes, we obtain: $ \text{Reg}_T \le O\left(\min\left\{\sqrt{dT\ln T},\; \frac{dT\ln T}{k|1-2δ|}\right\}\right) $, which is tight (up to logarithmic factors in $T$) across $T$, $k$ and $δ$. Specifically regarding the noise parameter $δ\in [0,1]$, the regret guarantee smoothly degrades as the oracle response approaches a coin flip, i.e., $δ$ is close to $\frac{1}{2}$. When applying the same techniques to a finite $K$ for the prediction with $d$ experts setting, the resulting rates are instead completely tight in all parameters, including $d$.
  Our analysis gives a streamlined treatment of pairwise probing in OCO by quantifying the benefit of probing via a variance reduction effect, combined with a second-order (variance-based) analysis of Continuous Exponential Weights.

</details>


### [172] [Graph Structured Combinatorial Semi-Bandit with Nonlinear Reward Associations through Separable Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14650)
*Christoph Bauschmann,Setareh Maghsudi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了基于图因果奖励建模、再生核方法和泰勒近似的通用自适应策略，用于在非线性依赖的大规模互联数据中识别最优结构，具有次线性时间复杂度和线性空间复杂度，并通过理论分析和实验验证了其鲁棒性和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大规模互联数据中识别最优结构需要大量采样和计算资源，而学习数据中的非线性统计依赖关系虽然能提升效率，但非线性特性极大地增加了复杂度，亟需高效且鲁棒的通用策略。

Method: 提出了结合图因果奖励建模、解析再生核方法以及函数过程泰勒近似的通用自适应策略，并建立了随时间的次线性时间复杂度和数据量的线性空间复杂度的理论性能保证。

Result: 理论分析了算法对噪声干扰、模型渐进收敛和解空间不匹配等多种不确定性的鲁棒性；在合成数据集和真实交通数据集上的数值实验验证了实际有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架以极少的先验假设和条件依赖展现了广泛的适用性，并可通过多种改进适应特定或扩展场景，为处理非线性依赖的大规模数据优化问题提供了有效的理论工具和实践方案。

Abstract: The identification of optimal structures within vast arrays of interconnected data necessitates significant sampling- and computational effort. Learning and leveraging underlying signal dependencies can improve efficiency and predictive capabilities considerably, but the ubiquity of nonlinear statistical relations amplifies the complexity of such undertakings. In this paper, we develop novel generic and adaptive strategies equipped with routines for graph-based causal reward modeling, analytic reproducing kernel methods, and Taylor approximation of functional processes. We establish theoretical performance guarantees sublinear in time and linear in data volume over time. Our analyses cover robustness to a multitude of uncertainties arising from noise interference, gradual model convergence, and solution space mismatch. The framework's general appeal is substantiated by a minimalistic set of conditions or reliance on prior estimates, while various outlined modifications address specific or extended settings. To demonstrate practical effectiveness, we conduct numerical experiments using both benchmarked synthetic and real-world transportation datasets.

</details>


### [173] [Beyond task performance: Decoding bioacoustic embeddings with speech features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14662)
*Ines Nolasco,Jules Cauzinille,Marius Miron,Gagan Narula,Milad Alizadeh,Emmanuel Fernandez,Matthieu Geist,Ellen Gilsenan-McMahon,Olivier Pietquin,Emmanuel Chemla,Sara Keen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统揭示了预训练音频嵌入模型在生物声学中编码了哪些类语音声学特征，发现不同模型覆盖互补的声学空间，无单一模型能捕捉全部特征；响度特征最易恢复（R²=0.76），基频最难恢复（R²=0.33），并通过特征可恢复性与物种特征显著性交叉分析，提出了数据驱动的模型选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物声学中广泛使用预训练音频嵌入，但学界对这些模型编码了哪些声学特征、哪些特征对特定任务有用知之甚少，这阻碍了模型的透明性，也限制了向稀有物种或数据稀缺领域推广的能力。

Method: 使用88维eGeMAPS特征，涵盖六个分类群，分别应用线性和非线性回归探针（probing）来量化每个模型捕捉到的声学属性。

Result: 1）验证了"没有免费午餐"模式，无单一模型能覆盖完整特征空间；2）拼接嵌入（concatenated embedding）表现最佳，表明模型间声学空间覆盖互补；3）响度特征编码最好（R²=0.76），F0最难恢复（R²=0.33）。

Conclusion: 通过交叉参考特征可恢复性与逐物种特征显著性（NMI），得出了数据驱动的生物声学模型选择指导原则。

Abstract: Pretrained audio embeddings are standard in bioacoustics, yet little is known about which acoustic features these models encode, nor which are useful for a given task. This hinders transparency and limits extension to rare species or data-scarce domains. Here we reveal which speech-like features are encoded in bioacoustic representations. Using the 88~eGeMAPS features across six taxonomic groups, we apply linear and nonlinear regression probes to quantify which acoustic properties each model captures. Results confirm a ``no free lunch'' pattern: no single model captures the full feature space. A concatenated embedding achieves the highest performance, suggesting complementary acoustic space coverage across models. Loudness features are best encoded ($R^2 = 0.76$) while F0 is hardest to recover ($R^2 = 0.33$). By cross-referencing recoverability with per-species feature salience (NMI), we derive data-driven model selection guidance for bioacoustics.

</details>


### [174] [When to Write and When to Suppress: Route-Specialized Dual Adapters for Memory-Assisted Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14668)
*Yining Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种路由专用双适配器编辑器（Route-Specialized Dual-Adapter Editor），通过引入相关性路由器决定是否激活编辑记忆，并分别使用编辑适配器和局部性适配器处理编辑后和未编辑的查询，在三个基准上取得了最佳概率偏好准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识编辑系统需要在不破坏无关行为的情况下更新特定事实。现有方法主要关注如何写入编辑，但忽略了何时抑制编辑的问题，导致非直接相关查询的原始知识被错误覆盖。

Method: 提出双适配器架构：1）相关性路由器（Router）判断当前提示是否需要接收编辑记忆；2）被路由的提示使用编辑适配器（Edit Adapter）倾向于新对象；3）未被路由的非直接提示使用局部性适配器（Locality Adapter）保留或恢复原始对象偏好。编辑通过参数高效适配器在推理时从记忆存储器检索并修正模型的对象偏好。

Result: 在三个基准（CF、ZSRE、MQuAKE）上，使用Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Qwen3-8B模型，该方法在所有基准上均取得最佳概率偏好准确率：CF为0.8180，ZSRE为0.8946，MQuAKE为0.9922。消融实验表明增益主要来自编辑注入与离路由抑制的分离，而非单纯增加LoRA容量。

Conclusion: 编辑系统设计的关键不仅在于如何写入编辑，还在于何时抑制编辑。路由策略的最佳选择因数据集而异：CF适合词法神经路由器，ZSRE和MQuAKE更适合BGE嵌入路由。

Abstract: Knowledge editing systems must update selected facts while preserving nearby but irrelevant behavior. This paper studies this problem in a memory-assisted setting where an edit memory is retrieved at inference time and a parameter-efficient adapter corrects the model's object preference. We argue that the central design question is not only how to write an edit, but also when to suppress it. We introduce \method{}, a route-specialized dual-adapter editor. A relevance router first decides whether a prompt should receive an edit memory. Routed prompts use an edit adapter trained to prefer the new object over the original object; unrouted non-direct prompts use a separate locality adapter trained to preserve or restore the original-object preference. We evaluate \method{} on three 1,000-case protocols, \cf{}, \zsre{}, and \mquake{}, under the same memory protocol and two 7B/8B base models. On Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, \method{} obtains the best overall probability-preference accuracy on all three benchmarks: 0.8180 on \cf{}, 0.8946 on \zsre{}, and 0.9922 on \mquake{}. The same trend holds on Qwen3-8B. Router ablations show that the relevant memory boundary differs across datasets: a lexical neural router is safest on \cf{}, while BGE embedding routing is better on \zsre{} and \mquake{}. Component and module ablations show that the gain mainly comes from separating edit injection from off-route suppression rather than from simply increasing LoRA capacity.

</details>


### [175] [Compressed Computation is (probably) not Computation in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14673)
*Jai Bhagat,Sara Molas-Medina,Giorgi Giglemiani,Stefan Heimersheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究压缩计算（CC）玩具模型是否属于叠加计算的实例。结果表明，CC模型通过噪声残差流混合输入，对应标签中非预期的混合矩阵。性能增益与混合矩阵的大小相关，当去除该矩阵时增益消失。学习到的神经元方向集中在混合矩阵前50个特征值对应的子空间。基于混合矩阵的SNMF基线可以复现定性损失曲线。这些结果表明CC不是合适的叠加计算玩具模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Braun等人提出的压缩计算（CC）玩具模型是否真正属于叠加计算（computation in superposition）的实例，即是否通过神经元的叠加利用实现了高效计算。

Method: 将训练目标分解为ReLU项和混合项，分析混合矩阵的影响；通过消融实验验证性能增益来源；检查神经元方向与混合矩阵特征空间的关系；使用半非负矩阵分解（SNMF）作为基线进行比较。

Result: CC模型的性能增益主要来自输入混合而非真正的叠加计算；神经元方向集中在混合矩阵前50个特征值子空间；基于混合矩阵的SNMF基线能复现定性损失曲线，但性能仍不及原始模型。

Conclusion: CC模型不是合适的叠加计算玩具模型，其表现出的"计算"能力实际上源于输入混合的伪影，而非真正的神经网络叠加计算机制。

Abstract: We study whether the Compressed Computation (CC) toy model (Braun et al., 2025) is an instance of computation in superposition. The CC model appears to compute 100 ReLU functions with just 50 neurons, achieving a better loss than expected from only representing 50 ReLU functions. We show that the model mixes inputs via its noisy residual stream, corresponding to an unintended mixing matrix in the labels. Splitting the training objective into the ReLU term and the mixing term, we find that performance gains scale with the magnitude of the mixing matrix and vanish when the matrix is removed. The learned neuron directions concentrate in the subspace associated with the top 50 eigenvalues of the mixing matrix, suggesting that the mixing term governs the solution. Finally, a semi-non-negative matrix factorization (SNMF) baseline derived solely from the mixing matrix reproduces the qualitative loss profile and improves on prior baselines, though it does not match the trained model. These results suggest CC is not a suitable toy model of computation in superposition.

</details>


### [176] [A Complexity Measure for Active Learning in Multi-group Mean Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14690)
*Abdellah Aznag,Rachel Cummings,Adam N. Elmachtoub*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多组均值估计d臂赌博机中的主动学习最大风险目标，提出了局部极小极大框架，给出了首个普适下界，并引入VLC复杂度度量，其下界由预算项、异方差指数和VLC三项因子构成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动学习/多臂赌博机理论多聚焦于平均性能，缺乏对最坏情况组别不确定性的系统性理解。本文旨在建立多组均值估计中最小化最大风险（max-risk）任务的理论下界，并揭示其性能瓶颈的根源因素。

Method: 采用局部极小极大框架，将下界分解为预算、异方差指数和方差局部曲率（VLC）三部分；引入损失诱导的ℓ1决策空间几何和基于表示（representation）的实例生成器，将难实例构造转化为显式随机矩阵计算。

Result: 证明了首个适用于任意有限方差假设类的max-risk下界；对光滑类，VLC可重新参数化为方差- Fisher信息量，常见分布族有封闭形式解；与最强上界对比，在广泛场景下达到对数因子内的近最优性，但在高度异质实例中存在系统性差距。

Conclusion: 本文建立的局部极小极大下界框架揭示了多组均值估计中最小化最大风险的本质困难由三个正交因素决定，为主动学习策略设计提供了理论基准，并指出了当前上界在高度异质场景下的改进空间。

Abstract: We study a \emph{max-risk} objective for active learning in a multi-group mean estimation $d$-armed bandits: a learner adaptively allocates a budget of $T$ samples across $d$ groups to minimize the worst-case uncertainty index $\max_{k\in[d]}σ_k^2/n_k$, where $σ_k$ is the standard deviation of the distribution of arm $d$, and $n_k$ is the number of times arm $d$ is sampled. We develop a local minimax framework and prove the first general lower bound for this objective, valid for any finite-variance hypothesis class. The bound separates difficulty into three orthogonal factors: a \emph{budget} term, a \emph{heteroscedasticity} index measuring how unevenly the uncertainty is spread across arms, and a model-dependent complexity measure, the \emph{Variance Local Curvature} ($\mathrm{VLC}$), which captures how much information a local change of variance creates inside the hypothesis class. For smooth classes, the $\mathrm{VLC}$ is a reparametrization of a variance--Fisher information, with closed-form values for common families. Benchmarking against the strongest available upper bound shows near-optimality up to logarithmic factors in broad regimes, and pinpoints a systematic gap in highly heterogeneous instances. Our proof introduces two key ingredients: a loss-induced $\ell_1$ geometry on the decision space, and a representation-based instance generator that reduces hard-instance construction to an explicit random matrix calculation.

</details>


<div id='math.OC'></div>

# math.OC [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Scalable Deep Unfolding of Conic Optimizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13825)
*Alex Oshin,Rahul Vodeb Ghosh,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出两种技术——矩阵无关的隐式微分（将内存从O(n²)降至O(n)）和基于Dalečkii–Krein表示的PSD锥反向传播（解决特征值重合时的数值不稳定问题）——使得可学习的大规模半定规划（SDP）求解器展开成为可能，在机器人学中的协方差规划、最大割、Lovász ϑ SDP、鲁棒估计与控制等问题上，相比COSMO等求解器实现高达50倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将深度展开（DU）应用于大规模半定规划（SDP）时，全更新锥求解器（如COSMO）的每轮迭代涉及两个关键障碍：一是线性系统求解的反向传播需显式构造系数矩阵，导致O(n²)内存开销，无法扩展到大规模问题；二是正半定（PSD）锥投影的反向传播在特征值重合时数值不稳定。现有学习型锥求解器未能解决这两个问题。

Method: 1) 提出矩阵无关的隐式微分规则，仅通过矩阵-向量乘积操作进行反向传播，将内存从O(n²)降至O(n)；2) 基于Dalečkii–Krein表示推导PSD锥的Frèchet导数反向传播规则，在重复特征值下保持良定义；3) 将这两种技术集成到全更新锥求解器（如COSMO）的展开框架中，学习轻量级超参数策略和热启动。

Result: 在非线性协方差规划（通过序列凸规划SCP实现）、最大割、Lovász ϑ SDP、鲁棒估计与控制等任务上，学习到的策略在所有问题上均优于最先进的求解器，根据问题类别可实现高达50倍加速；作为SCP子程序时，相比COSMO实现超过30倍加速。

Conclusion: 所提出的矩阵无关隐式微分和稳定的PSD锥反向传播技术有效克服了深度展开应用于大规模SDP的两大障碍，使得学习型锥求解器在机器人学典型的大规模SDP问题上兼具计算效率和数值稳定性，显著超越传统求解器。

Abstract: Deep unfolding (DU) accelerates iterative optimizers by introducing learnable components and training them through unrolled iterations, but extending DU to the large-scale semidefinite programs (SDPs) common in robotics has remained limited. Unrolling a full-update conic solver such as COSMO exposes two obstacles that prior work on learned conic solvers has not: backpropagating through the per-iteration linear-system solve incurs memory quadratic in the problem size once the coefficient matrix is formed explicitly, and backpropagating through the positive semidefinite (PSD) cone projection becomes numerically unstable when eigenvalues coincide. We address the first obstacle with a matrix-free implicit differentiation rule that operates entirely through matrix-vector products, reducing memory from $O(n^2)$ to $O(n)$ and enabling backpropagation at scales where direct factorization runs out of memory. We address the second with a backward rule based on the Dalečkii--Krein representation of the Fréchet derivative, which remains well-defined under repeated eigenvalues. Together these make it possible to learn lightweight hyperparameter policies and warm-starts for a full-update conic solver. We evaluate on nonlinear covariance steering problems solved via sequential convex programming (SCP), as well as standalone SDPs and second-order cone programs ranging from max-cut and Lovász $\vartheta$ SDPs to robust estimation and control problems. The learned policies outperform state-of-the-art solvers across all problems, and can provide up to a 50$\times$ speedup depending on the class. When used as a subroutine in SCP, the learned approach delivers over a 30$\times$ speedup compared to COSMO.

</details>


### [178] [Delay-dependent passivity and stability of linear port-Hamiltonian systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13897)
*Ikram El Haskouki,Hannes Gernandt*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了含时滞项的线性端口-哈密顿系统的时滞依赖与独立无源性特性，利用特定存储函数和Wirtinger不等式推导无源性条件，并回顾了时滞依赖与独立的指数稳定性条件，最后将结果应用于区域供热网络和含时滞阻尼/刚度的振荡器模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端口-哈密顿系统广泛应用于物理系统的建模，但实际系统中常存在时滞现象（如通信延迟、传输延迟等），而时滞会破坏系统的无源性和稳定性。因此，需要系统性地研究时滞对端口-哈密顿系统无源性和稳定性的影响，并建立可判定的条件。

Method: 采用特定存储函数（Lyapunov-Krasovskii泛函）结合Wirtinger不等式来推导时滞依赖和时滞独立无源性条件；同时回顾了利用线性矩阵不等式（LMI）框架表述的时滞依赖与独立指数稳定性判据。

Result: 给出了时滞依赖和时滞独立两类无源性条件，以及相应的指数稳定性条件；这些条件均以线性矩阵不等式的形式给出，便于数值验证。

Conclusion: 所得无源性和稳定性条件可有效应用于区域供热网络模型和含时滞阻尼/刚度的振荡器系统，为含时滞端口-哈密顿系统的分析与控制提供了理论工具。

Abstract: We study delay-dependent and independent passivity properties of linear port-Hamiltonian systems with delay terms. We derive passivity conditions using a particular storage function and Wirtinger-based inequalities. Furthermore, we recall several conditions for delay-dependent and independent exponential stability. Finally, we present an application of our passivity and stability results to delay equations arising in the modeling of district heating networks and oscillators with delayed damping and stiffness.

</details>


### [179] [Proof and More Variations of Bellman's Lost-in-a-forest Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13987)
*Zhipeng Deng*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在已有研究基础上，给出了Bellman"迷失在森林中"问题的通用解证明，并获得了更多变体与结果，建立了曲线覆盖、迷失森林问题与带邻域旅行商问题之间的通用形式化方法，证明了等价性与收敛性，同时提供了两条线搜索、Wetzel单位弧覆盖问题、闭环路径变体、三维变体等扩展结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在已有通用公式与计算方法基础上，进一步为Bellman迷失森林问题提供严格证明，并探索该问题的更多变体形式，建立其与其他经典优化问题之间的理论联系。

Method: 采用广义形式化方法，将曲线覆盖问题、迷失森林问题与带邻域旅行商问题统一建模，证明三者之间的等价性与收敛性。通过推导通用计算方程，结合部分解析分析与数值计算，求解多种变体问题。

Result: 给出了通用解的理论证明；获得了多条线的搜索结果；建立了与Wetzel单位弧覆盖问题的联系；得到了闭环路径变体与三维变体的结果；提供了通用计算方程、部分解析结果及数值结果。

Conclusion: 本文成功推广了Bellman迷失森林问题的理论框架，证明了其与多个经典几何优化问题的内在联系，并通过解析和数值手段获得了丰富的变体结果。

Abstract: In this paper, based on our previous general formulation and computational solution to Bellman's Lost-in-a-forest Problem, we provide the proof of general solution and obtained more variations and results related to this problem. This paper provides generalized formalized method connecting curve covering, lost-in-the-forest problem, and traveling salesman problem with neighborhoods. We prove the equivalence and convergence. We also provide more results of searching for two lines, connection to Wetzel's unit arc covering problem, variations with closed path, variations in three dimensions, etc. The results include general calculation equations, partial analytical results, and numerical results.

</details>


### [180] [A Semismooth Newton-Based Proximal Augmented Lagrangian Method for Joint Estimation of Multiple Gaussian Graphical Models with Clustered Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14041)
*Han Wang,Yue Liu,Yong-Jin Liu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类凸复合优化问题，用于联合估计聚类多个高斯图模型。提出了一种近端增广拉格朗日方法结合半光滑牛顿法，利用块对角结构分解大规模问题，相比一阶方法具有更快的收敛速度和更好的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在聚类多个高斯图模型的联合估计中，存在大规模凸复合优化问题需要高效求解，现有方法计算复杂度高。

Method: 首先建立了解为块对角结构的充要条件以实现问题分解；然后开发了近端增广拉格朗日方法，每个子问题通过超线性收敛的半光滑牛顿法求解。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的实验表明，该方法相比现有最先进方法具有更高的效率和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提方法通过充分利用二阶信息（半光滑牛顿框架）实现更快收敛和更好鲁棒性，优于广泛使用的一阶方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a class of convex composite optimization problems arising from the joint estimation of clustered multiple Gaussian graphical models. The resulting model combines a log-determinant loss term with a nonsmooth sparse clustered regularizer, which encourages both similar sparsity patterns and consistent edge values across different graphs. We first establish a necessary and sufficient condition under which the solution is block diagonal, enabling a large-scale problem to be decomposed into smaller independent subproblems and substantially reducing computational complexity. To efficiently solve this problem, we develop a proximal augmented Lagrangian method in which each subproblem is handled by a superlinearly convergent semismooth Newton method. Unlike widely used first order methods, our approach fully exploits the underlying second order information through the semismooth Newton framework, thereby achieving faster convergence and improved robustness. The efficiency and robustness of the proposed algorithm are demonstrated through comparisons with state-of-the-art methods on both synthetic and real data sets.

</details>


### [181] [Battery Bidding under Price Uncertainty in Wholesale Electricity Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14050)
*Vincent Yinjun-Wang,Madeleine Udell*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文建立了考虑价格不确定性和风险管理的电池储能在日前市场中的报价模型，证明可通过线性规划精确求解，并揭示了即使没有市场势力，储能在稀缺状态下的 withholding 行为、不确定性对报价的非对称影响以及风险管理如何塑造多层次报价曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网规模电池在批发电力市场中的报价模式难以解释，尤其是看似战略性 withholding 的行为可能源于价格不确定性和风险管理下的理性运营，需要建立可解释的资产级模型来区分。

Method: 构建以平均-CVaR为目标、受物理和市场化约束的价格接受型电池日前市场阶梯报价模型，将原始混合整数线性规划转化为等价线性规划，实现精确求解。

Result: 1) 即使没有市场势力，稀缺储能和价值不确定性也会导致 withholding；2) 不确定性对报价的影响取决于荷电状态：稀缺时提升卖价，充裕时降低卖价；3) 风险管理使报价曲线呈现分层结构，确保广泛场景下盈利的同时保留对稀有高价的暴露。

Conclusion: 电池储能 observed bidding 中出现的 withholding 行为不一定反映市场操纵，而可能是价格不确定性和风险规避下的理性结果，为监管者解读市场行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Grid-scale batteries increasingly influence outcomes in wholesale electricity markets, but their observed bid patterns remain difficult to interpret. In particular, bids that appear to reflect strategic withholding may instead arise from rational operations under price uncertainty and risk management. We develop an asset-level model of a price-taking battery that submits stepwise buy and sell bid curves in the day-ahead market under a finite set of price scenarios. The battery chooses quantity--price pairs to maximize a mean--CVaR objective subject to physical and market constraints. A direct formulation is a mixed-integer linear program, but we show that its integer decisions can be removed, yielding an exact linear programming reformulation suitable for empirical analysis. Our empirical results deliver three insights. First, withholding behavior can arise even without market power, because scarce stored energy and uncertain future prices increase the value of holding energy. Second, the effect of uncertainty depends on the state of charge: when stored energy is scarce, greater uncertainty raises sell bid prices, whereas when stored energy is abundant it can lower them. Third, risk management reshapes bid curves into layered structures that secure profitable execution across a broad set of scenarios while preserving some exposure to rare but valuable price spikes.

</details>


### [182] [A New Primal-Dual Algorithm with Convex Combination and Extrapolation for Convex-Concave Saddle Point Problems with Nonlinear Coupling Term](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14220)
*Jialong Li,Zexian Liu,Xiaokai Chang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对带非线性耦合项的凸-凹鞍点问题，提出了一种新的原始-对偶算法PDAce及其加速版本aPDAce。核心创新在于在凸组合点处对非线性耦合项中的向量函数进行线性化，并将外推从变量空间转移到非线性映射空间，从而消除了非线性残差项并实现了无需线搜索的严格收敛分析。在一般凸假设下达到O(1/N)的遍历收敛率，在强凸条件下加速版本可实现O(1/N^2)甚至线性收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的原始-对偶算法在处理带非线性耦合项的凸-凹鞍点问题时，存在变量空间与映射空间之间的几何不匹配、梯度信息延迟以及强依赖于线搜索等问题，导致性能不稳定且收敛分析复杂。本文旨在解决这些痛点。

Method: 提出PDAce算法：在原始变量更新中，构造凸组合点代替当前迭代点，并在该点计算非线性耦合项中向量函数的雅可比矩阵；在对偶变量更新中，利用凸组合点的最新信息在映射空间进行外推。核心创新包括：(1) 在凸组合点处对非线性耦合项的向量函数进行线性化；(2) 将外推从变量空间转移到非线性映射空间。加速版本aPDAce在此基础上进一步实现更快的收敛速率。

Result: PDAce在一般凸假设下具有全局收敛性，遍历收敛率为O(1/N)；aPDAce在原始函数强凸时达到O(1/N^2)的收敛率，当原始和对偶函数均强凸时达到线性收敛。

Conclusion: 通过凸组合和外推策略的结合，PDAce有效消除了非线性残差项，实现了无需线搜索的严格收敛分析，为解决带非线性耦合项的凸-凹鞍点问题提供了一种更稳定、理论更完善的原始-对偶算法框架。

Abstract: Convex-concave saddle point problems with nonlinear coupling term have wide applications in signal processing, machine learning, robust optimization, and generative models, among others. Primal-dual algorithms are widely used for convex-concave saddle point problems. However, when handling nonlinear coupling term in convex-concave saddle point problems, primal-dual algorithms usually encounter geometric mismatches between variable and mapping spaces, delayed gradient information, and strong dependence on linesearch, resulting in less stable performance and complex convergence analysis. To address these issues, we propose a new primal-dual algorithm named PDAce by combining convex combination and extrapolation strategies. More specifically, in the update of the primal variable, we construct a convex combination point to replace the current iterative point and compute the Jacobian matrix of the vector function in the nonlinear coupling term at the convex combination point. Besides, we use the latest information of convex combination points to extend extrapolation to the mapping space in the update of the dual variable. The core innovations lie in performing linearization of the vector function in the nonlinear coupling term at convex combination points and shifting extrapolation from the variable space to the nonlinear mapping space. This design completely eliminates nonlinear residual terms and allows for rigorous convergence analysis without linesearch. Under mild convex assumptions, we construct a new Lyapunov potential function to prove that PDAce is globally convergent with an ergodic convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}(1/N)$. Moreover, we develop an accelerated version of PDAce, termed aPDAce, which achieves $\mathcal{O}(1/N^2)$ rate under strong convexity of the primal function, and linear convergence when both the primal and dual functions are strongly convex.

</details>


### [183] [Operator Calculus for Population-Based Optimization: A Mean-Field Convergence Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14289)
*Pekka Malo,Lauri Viitasaari,Patrik Nummi,Antti Suominen,Ankur Sinha,Olli Tahvonen*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种算子微积分框架，将基于种群和分布的优化方法统一描述为三个基本算子（变异、选择、重组）的复合作用，并建立了模块化Lyapunov原理，在连续时间极限下得到输运-反应-跳跃（TRJ）PDE，为复合平均场算法的指数收敛性提供了系统化的验证工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于种群和分布的优化方法（如进化策略、共识优化、协方差矩阵适应、随机梯度方法等）被广泛应用于非凸或黑箱问题，但其收敛性分析分散在各类算法特定的技术中，缺乏统一的理论框架。

Method: 引入算子微积分，将优化方法描述为三个基本算子（变异、选择、重组）作用于概率测度的复合；通过适当的状态空间选择和状态增强（记忆或策略变量），在显式稳定性和正则性条件下，复合算子具有预生成元，其连续时间极限为保持算子分裂的输运-反应-跳跃（TRJ）PDE。在此基础上建立模块化Lyapunov原理。

Result: 若状态空间Lyapunov函数在全生成元下耗散且控制相关搜索空间度量，则状态空间Lyapunov泛函及诱导的搜索误差呈指数衰减。加性生成元结构允许逐算子组装耗散估计，为复合平均场算法的收敛性认证提供工具包。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了多种基于种群和分布的优化方法的收敛性分析，提供了模块化的收敛性认证工具，有望推广到更广泛的算法类。

Abstract: Population-based and distributional optimization methods, from evolution strategies and consensus-based optimization to covariance-matrix adaptation and stochastic gradient methods viewed as distributional dynamics, are widely used for nonconvex or black-box problems, yet their convergence analyses remain fragmented across algorithm-specific techniques. We introduce an operator calculus in which a broad class of such methods, after choosing an appropriate state space and, where necessary, augmenting the state by memory or strategy variables, is described as a composition of three elementary operators (mutation, selection, and recombination) acting on probability measures. Under explicit stability and regularity conditions, the composite operator admits a pre-generator whose continuous-time limit is a transport-reaction-jump (TRJ) PDE that preserves the operator splitting. On this foundation we establish a modular Lyapunov principle. If a state-space Lyapunov function both dissipates under the full generator and controls the relevant search-space gauges, then the state-space Lyapunov functional and the induced search errors decay exponentially. The additive generator structure allows dissipation estimates to be assembled operator by operator, providing a toolkit for certifying convergence of composite mean-field algorithms.

</details>


### [184] [A simulation study on spatial exponential decay of perturbations in a two-dimensional wave equation with optimal boundary/line control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14410)
*Benedikt Oppeneiger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文通过二维波动方程仿真研究表明，只有控制域均匀分布（如内部线控制网格）才能实现最优边界控制中扰动的域一致空间指数衰减，而仅边界控制无法达到这一效果，原因在于有限传播速度要求控制域间距存在上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维双曲型PDE中已证明域一致可镇定性和可检测性可实现扰动空间指数衰减，且这要求控制域均匀分布。本文探究高维最优边界控制中是否也存在类似效应。

Method: 对二维波动方程在扩展方形区域上进行仿真，比较两种控制几何构型：仅外边界控制 vs 边界结合规则内部线界面网格控制。采用P1有限元空间离散、隐式中点法时间离散，通过凝聚公式简化KKT系统，用预处理共轭梯度法求解缩减后的最优性系统。

Result: 仅当控制域包含规则内部线网格时才能观测到扰动的域一致空间衰减；仅外边界控制无法实现该衰减。

Conclusion: 高维最优边界控制中要实现扰动空间指数衰减，同样需要控制域在空间上均匀分布（间距有上界），这源于有限传播速度的物理约束。

Abstract: Recent results have shown that domain-uniform stabilizability and detectability imply spatial exponential decay of perturbations in optimally controlled hyperbolic PDEs on one-dimensional domains. This domain-uniform stabilizability and detectability can be achieved only if the control domain is distributed over the whole spatial domain such that the distance between neighboring control intervals is bounded from above. Motivated by these insights we investigate whether analogous effects can be observed for optimal boundary control in higher dimensions. For this purpose we conduct a simulation study on a two-dimensional wave equation on expanding square domains which is driven by a localized perturbation of the initial displacement. We compare two control geometries: a control acting only on the outer boundary and a control acting on a regular grid of interior line interfaces combined with the boundary. The problem is discretized by conforming P1 finite elements in space and the implicit midpoint rule in time. Instead of assembling the full space-time KKT system, we use a condensed formulation and solve the reduced optimality system by preconditioned conjugate gradients. We show that domain-uniform spatial decay of the perturbation can only be observed in the scenario with a regular grid of line controls. This is because finite propagation velocity requires a uniform bound on the distance that a wave can travel without reaching the control domain.

</details>


### [185] [Free Heavy-Tailed Lunch for Muon: A Theoretical Justification of Empirical Success](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14560)
*Florian Hübler,Thomas Pethick,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了非欧优化方法（如Muon）在重尾非凸随机优化中，对于矩阵参数达到核范数ε-驻点的最优样本复杂度为O(min{m,n} Δ₁L/ε² · (σ/ε)^{p/(p-1)})，避免了欧几里得方法的额外维度依赖，并在大语言模型实验中验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非欧优化方法（Muon、Scion）在Transformer训练中表现优异，但其理论优势（尤其相对于欧几里得方法）尚不清晰。作者希望填补这一理论空白，特别是在重尾非凸随机优化场景下。

Method: 在随机梯度满足p阶中心矩有界（p∈(1,2]）的重尾非凸假设下，分析非欧优化方法的样本复杂度；推导了Muon在核范数平稳性下的最优样本复杂度下界；通过大语言模型实验验证理论。

Result: Muon达到核范数ε-驻点的样本复杂度为O(min{m,n} Δ₁L/ε² · (σ/ε)^{p/(p-1)})，该复杂度对所有一阶方法都是最优的；且非欧方法避免了欧几里得方法依赖维度的额外代价。

Conclusion: 非欧优化方法在重尾非凸优化中具有严格的理论优势，且其他Schatten范数几何（不限于Muon的谱几何）在特定场景下也能取得竞争性能。

Abstract: Non-Euclidean optimisation methods with matrix-valued updates, such as Muon and Scion, have recently shown strong empirical performance for training Transformer models, yet their theoretical advantages over Euclidean methods remain poorly understood. We address this gap in the heavy-tailed non-convex regime, where stochastic gradients have bounded $p$-th central moments, $p \in (1,2]$. We show that certain non-Euclidean methods achieve optimal sample complexity under stronger stationarity measures, while Euclidean methods incur additional dimension-dependent costs. As a consequence, for $m \times n$ matrices, Muon finds an $\varepsilon$-stationary point in nuclear norm within $\mathcal{O}\left(\min\{m, n\} \frac{Δ_1 L}{\varepsilon^2} \left(\frac σ\varepsilon \right)^{\frac p {p-1}}\right)$ samples, absorbing heavy-tailed noise without extra dimension dependence, unlike Euclidean methods. We further prove this sample complexity, including its dimension dependence, is optimal for all first-order methods under nuclear-norm stationarity. Experiments on large language models support our theory. Surprisingly, our results suggest that other Schatten geometries beyond the spectral geometry of Muon can perform competitively in certain settings.

</details>
